Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in biological theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 589 and the invariant I(A,B) = 723 mod 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 589 + O(1/n^723). Summing over all modes and applying the biological convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
